445580	CC/C=C\\C/C=C\\C/C=C\\C/C=C\\C/C=C\\C/C=C\\CCC(=O)O	The molecule is a docosahexaenoic acid having six cis-double bonds at positions 4, 7, 10, 13, 16 and 19. It has a role as a nutraceutical, an antineoplastic agent, a human metabolite, a Daphnia tenebrosa metabolite, a mouse metabolite and an algal metabolite. It is a docosahexaenoic acid and an omega-3 fatty acid.
